Transfer","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_48342d4c51fe6b989251","text":"Goods and obligations move between parties through ownership, valuation, replacement, compensation, gift, or offering.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:The Marked and Straight Route","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_4886afa6877bfde9d14a","text":"Movement is organized by a course, a guide, and a state of passage, then tested by deviation, engulfment, or loss of mobility.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Patronage, Support, and Claimed Recognition","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_4a07c27ad4b40bf16162","text":"Good is conferred through compassion and maintenance, stabilized as welfare, and answered by praise or recognition.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:The Marked and Straight Route","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_4b4d4e27d8cc2c6f0f06","text":"1:2 `ٱلْعَٰلَمِينَ` (`ع ل م`); 1:4 `مَٰلِكِ` and `ٱلدِّينِ` (`م ل ك`, `د ي ن`); 1:5 `نَعْبُدُ` (`ع ب د`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا ٱلصِّرَٰطَ ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ه د ي`, `ص ر ط`, `ق و م`); 1:7 `صِرَٰطَ` (`ص ر ط`)","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:The Momentous Day of Rising and 1:4 `يَوْمِ` (`ي و م`); 1:5 `نَعْبُدُ` (`ع ب د`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا` (`ه د ي`); 1:7 `أَنْعَمْتَ` (`ن ع م`)","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Cohesion, Mainstay, and Upright Members","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_60a5e75ed45312b1fa21","text":"A structure or undertaking remains standing because material cohesion, a firm joint, and upright supporting members carry its load.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Daylight, Zenith, and Celestial Marking","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_6112a41ef99ec31a4ad0","text":"A daylight span is located by the sun at zenith and by visible signs in the overhead sky.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000552/B002","root_001444/B004","root_001583/B004","root_001583/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Marriage and Bride-Conveyance","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_64778479b095a5bc77ca","text":"marriage contract `م ل ك:B004/m01`; bride conveyed to her husband `ه د ي:B006/m01`; kinship relation `ر ح م:B002/m01`; affectionate gift `ه د ي:B004/m01`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:The Visible Leader at the Front","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_65920a8d1a4a79e0381e","text":"Movement is organized by a course, a guide, and a state of passage, then tested by deviation, engulfment, or loss of mobility.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000552/B001","root_001444/B002","root_001525/B001","root_001583/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Reciprocal Gift and Beneficent Transfer","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_65c89f255a67c6964e92","text":"affectionate gift-giving `ه د ي:B004/m01`; brief delay `ع ب د:B009/m01`; quick running `ع ب د:B009/m02`; increase or intensification `ن ع م:B010/m01`; the utmost praised goal `ح م د:B004/m01`; calm bearing `ه د ي:B010/m01`; continued residence `ر ب ب:B007/m01`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Assent, Certification, and Qualification","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_696f31ed4be1e396d02b","text":"1:2 `رَبِّ` (`ر ب ب`); 1:4 `ٱلدِّينِ` (`د ي ن`); 1:7 `غَيْرِ` and `أَنْعَمْتَ` (`غ ي ر`, `ن ع م`)","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Governed and Pleasant Settlement","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_6b9c8e427a66259d4601","text":"Habitation becomes viable when authority, residence, water, weather, and vegetation converge into a sustaining place.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Patronage, Support, and Claimed Recognition","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_6c25e5d6fa27cdbe975f","text":"1:2 `ٱلْحَمْدُ` and `رَبِّ` (`ح م د`, `ر ب ب`); the leading animal followed by the group `م ل ك:B008/m01`; an elevated visible figure `س م و:B002/m01`; a gathered wild-cattle herd `ر ب ب:B014/m01`; a herd of wild donkeys `ع و ن:B006/m01`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Marriage and Bride-Conveyance","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_7ed044d644e49996608d","text":"Contract creates the relation, conveyance enacts the transition between households, kinship names the resulting bond, and the gift supplies a reciprocal social gesture around the transfer.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Herd, Livestock, and the Stray Margin","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_82311ef6c2eaec68be91","text":"Species and age make animals classifiable, gathering makes them a herd, and the lead animal provides internal order. The stray defines the boundary case where an animal loses both group placement and known ownership.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:The Visible Leader at the Front","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_829ed2415e1d7ef34a1b","text":"A conspicuous leader takes the foremost position and draws a herd or procession behind it.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Water-Secured Encampment and Livelihood","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_83700bc53471e97557b8","text":"The well or collected water supplies the resource, control of water makes encampment possible, irrigation extends it into the surrounding land, and livelihood is the resulting stable state.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B007","root_000532/B015","root_001119/B005","root_001525/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Assent, Certification, and Qualification","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_851418494cc6d78e4329","text":"affirmative response `ن ع م:B004/m01`;
